- **charmander-build**: Build-validate phase. Implement the next unblocked ticket test-first on a local branch and validate it with the ticket's tests and the repo's checks, recording evidence. No PR and, by default, no review here; /charmander-review opens the PR next. Use after the plan gate passes. ([richardkabiling/loop-test-repo](https://github.com/richardkabiling/loop-test-repo/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/charmander-build))
- **charmander-design**: Design phase. Turn an approved intent.md into spec.md (requirements with EARS acceptance criteria) plus the design subphases this work item needs (requirements, solution, api, data, infra, ux, docs), each reviewed by fresh-context personas, then the design gate. Use after the intent gate passes. ([richardkabiling/loop-test-repo](https://github.com/richardkabiling/loop-test-repo/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/charmander-design))
- **charmander-improve**: Improve phase. Turn signals, incidents and session lessons into classified findings (product, phase, harness, convention, code-part convention or no-op) and ship the smallest evidence-backed change for each, with research and evals. Modes: retro, incident, tune, scan. Use after a work item ships, after an incident, on the weekly signals report, or after a model or tool upgrade. ([richardkabiling/loop-test-repo](https://github.com/richardkabiling/loop-test-repo/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/charmander-improve))
- **charmander-intent**: Intent phase. Turn an idea, request or signal into an approved intent.md by interviewing the originator (problem, outcome, users, constraints, open questions). - **charmander-plan**: Plan phase. Turn an approved spec.md into plan.md (files, order, risks, proof) and tracer-bullet tickets with blocking edges, approved by an engineer. - **charmander-review**: Review phase. Open the PR for a validated ticket, compute its tier, run the automated checks, AI reviews and context-less persona lenses that tier calls for, fix majors red-first on the same PR, answer every finding, and request the tier's human approvers. Use after /charmander-build validates a ticket. ([richardkabiling/loop-test-repo](https://github.com/richardkabiling/loop-test-repo/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/charmander-review))
